        Idea:
vibecomfy: warm-runtime sessions (keep models resident across generations by reusing Comfy's existing model_management, not reinventing it).

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy
Embedded ComfyUI fork in use: hiddenswitch (path: /Users/user_c042661f/.cache/uv/git-v0/checkouts/b790d0c34622492a/c5ed9402/)

# Problem
Today both vibecomfy runtime paths are cold-per-call:
- Embedded: vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125-138 enters/exits a fresh Comfy() context per run. The exit calls embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup -> model_management.unload_all_models() + gc + soft_empty_cache. Models are dumped after every generation.
- Managed server: vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:16 starts comfyui as a subprocess, queues once, terminates. Same outcome at the process level.

ComfyUI itself already keeps models warm by default — current_loaded_models persists in process, load_models_gpu reuses resident copies, eviction is pressure-driven, OOM is auto-handled (execution.py:735-737), and DISABLE_SMART_MEMORY is the only thing that forces unload after each prompt. So the work is structural: stop tearing the runtime down.

# Goal
Add session-aware warm runtime to vibecomfy with two backends sharing one abstraction:
1. Long-lived managed Comfy server (HTTP control plane) — default for CLI users.
2. Long-lived embedded Comfy() context — for in-process callers (runpod worker, tests, tight matrix loops).

Default policy: keep warm. Explicit flush available. Auto-flush on detected model-set change under VRAM pressure.

# Verified facts (don't re-research)
- POST /free and POST /api/free both exist in the embedded hiddenswitch fork at comfy/cmd/server.py:1041 with /api mirror auto-added at :1357. Payload: {"unload_models": bool, "free_memory": bool}. They set queue flags via prompt_queue.set_flag(...) — async, take effect at next prompt boundary, not synchronously.
- CLI flags exist in comfy/cli_args.py:115-160: --highvram, --lowvram, --normalvram, --reserve-vram <GB>, --disable-smart-memory, --cache-classic, --cache-lru N, --cache-none.
- Comfy.clear_cache() (embedded_comfy_client.py:385) and Comfy.reconfigure() (:398) already exposed; reconfigure uses MODEL_MANAGEMENT_ARGS fingerprint to avoid unnecessary subprocess cycling.
- The cleanup that nukes warm state lives in embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup (lines ~149-169) and is called from Comfy.__aexit__ (line ~481).

# Design constraints / refinements over the user's notes
- Do NOT default to --highvram (breaks multi-model workflows that need offload). Default to Comfy's smart memory.
- /free is asynchronous — flush API must either drain via a no-op prompt or document the queue-flag semantics. Tests must account for this.
- Auto-flush should only trigger on model-set fingerprint change AND VRAM-headroom-below-threshold. Input/seed/prompt changes must NOT trigger flush.
- VIBECOMFY_WARM=auto|always|never controls policy (auto default).
- Session lifecycle: daemon-style for CLI (pidfile + URL file under out/sessions/<id>/) so multiple `vibecomfy run` invocations share one warm pool. Embedded session is just a Python context manager.
- Browser-only tasks: there are none in this plan (no UI work).
- No hard-halt prereq clauses: each milestone is independently shippable.

# Out of scope for this megaplan
- Multi-GPU session pooling.
- Distributed/remote session sharing across machines.
- Workflow-graph-level caching (Comfy already has --cache-lru / --cache-classic).
- Replacing the existing one-shot run/run_embedded APIs — they should remain as thin wrappers around the new session.
- Cloud/RunPod orchestration changes (the runpod worker can adopt EmbeddedSession in a separate plan).

# Suggested milestone shape (megaplan can refine during prep/plan)
M1: Session abstraction + embedded backend
  - vibecomfy/runtime/session.py: VibeSession protocol + EmbeddedSession holding a long-lived Comfy() across multiple .run(workflow) calls.
  - .flush() -> Comfy.clear_cache(); .reconfigure(config) -> Comfy.reconfigure().
  - Refactor run_embedded() to be a one-shot wrapper around EmbeddedSession.
  - Tests: two sequential runs in one session reuse models (assert via inspecting comfy.model_management.current_loaded_models after each run, or via a stub model manager); flush() drops them; reconfigure with same fingerprint is a no-op.

M2: Managed server backend
  - ServerSession: keeps comfy_server subprocess alive across .run() calls. Start = spawn + wait-ready. .flush() = POST /api/free with appropriate flags. .stop() = SIGTERM with timeout fallback to SIGKILL.
  - Daemon mode: `vibecomfy session start [--id NAME] [--vram-policy ...]` writes pidfile + URL to out/sessions/<id>/; `vibecomfy session stop <id>`; `vibecomfy session list`; `vibecomfy session flush <id>`.
  - `vibecomfy run` auto-discovers active session if one exists, else falls back to one-shot managed mode.
  - Tests: session start/stop, flush via HTTP, two runs share warmth, daemon survives across CLI invocations.

M3: Auto-flush policy + fingerprinting
  - vibecomfy/runtime/model_fingerprint.py: extract loader-node identity tuples from a workflow (CheckpointLoaderSimple.ckpt_name, LoraLoader.lora_name, UNETLoader.unet_name, VAELoader.vae_name, CLIPLoader.clip_name, DualCLIPLoader, etc. — discover the canonical set from the embedded fork's nodes).
  - Session.run() compares current workflow fingerprint to last-run fingerprint. If different AND free VRAM < threshold (configurable, default e.g. 2GB), call flush() before queueing.
  - VIBECOMFY_WARM env var: auto (default) | always | never.
  - Tests: same model + different seed -> no flush; different ckpt -> flush; auto-flush gated by VRAM headroom (mock get_free_memory).

M4: VRAM/cache flag plumbing + docs
  - Session config accepts: vram_policy ("auto"|"high"|"low"|"normal"), reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy ("smart"|"classic"|"lru:N"|"none"), disable_smart_memory.
  - Threaded through to embedded Configuration AND server CLI args.
  - docs/runtime_lifecycle.md: add "Warm sessions" section. docs/runtime_surface.md: document VibeSession API.
  - Smoke test: `vibecomfy session start && vibecomfy run x.json && vibecomfy run y.json && vibecomfy session stop` — second run is materially faster than first (timing-based, with tolerance).

# Open questions for the planning step
- Should `vibecomfy run` lazily start a session on first call and reuse it (implicit warm) vs always require `session start`? Lean toward explicit start for predictability.
- Does the runpod worker (reigh-workspace/reigh-worker) want to consume EmbeddedSession directly or talk to a local managed session? Probably embedded — it owns the process lifecycle already. Out of scope for this plan, but the API should not preclude it.
- Do we need partial flush (just CLIP, just VAE, just UNet)? Probably no — Comfy's pressure-driven eviction handles per-model decisions; only expose all-or-nothing flush at the session boundary.

# Success criteria (suggested — planning phase should refine)
- A `VibeSession` abstraction exists with both Embedded and Server backends.
- Two sequential `.run()` calls in one session share warm models (verified by test, not just timing).
- `flush()` is explicit and works for both backends. For the server backend, the test accounts for /free's queue-flag semantics.
- `vibecomfy session start/stop/list/flush` CLI commands exist and survive across separate `vibecomfy` invocations.
- Auto-flush policy fires on model-set change under VRAM pressure, never on seed/input changes.
- Existing `run` and `run_embedded` one-shot APIs continue to work unchanged (backwards compatibility).
- Documentation updated in docs/runtime_lifecycle.md and docs/runtime_surface.md.

Tests in Step 7.3 expect typed-name daemon config carry-through; tests for the embedded path may also feed raw HS keys.

      "id": "T6",
      "description": "Create vibecomfy/commands/session.py with argparse for start/stop/list/flush/status AND in-process daemon entrypoint (single file). Daemon invocation: `python -m vibecomfy.commands.session --daemon --id NAME --config <json>`. Daemon body: `config_dict = json.loads(args.config); session = ServerSession(SessionConfig.from_dict(config_dict)); await session.start(); write out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}; install SIGTERM handler that calls await session.stop() and removes pidfile; sleep on asyncio.Event`. Daemonize from `session start` via `subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-m', 'vibecomfy.commands.session', '--daemon', '--id', id_, '--config', json.dumps(config)], stdout=DEVNULL, stderr=open(log_path,'ab'), start_new_session=True)`. Parent assembles config dict from CLI flags as TYPED-NAME dict matching SessionConfig field names (omit None values): `{port, vram_policy, reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy, warm_policy, disable_smart_memory}`. Parent waits up to 120 s for out/sessions/<id>/url. Subcommands: `start [--id NAME=default] [--vram-policy auto|high|low|normal] [--reserve-vram-gb F] [--cache-policy smart|classic|lru:N|none] [--disable-smart-memory] [--warm-policy auto|always|never] [--port P=8188]`, `stop <id>`, `list`, `flush <id> [--unload-models/--no-unload-models] [--free-memory/--no-free-memory]`, `status <id>`. Add `find_active_session(id='default') -> str | None` in runtime/session.py: read out/sessions/<id>/url; if pidfile present and os.kill(pid, 0) succeeds return URL else clean up and return None. Register CommandSpec('session', 'vibecomfy.commands.session') in vibecomfy/commands/__init__.py:14-28 BETWEEN 'runtime' and 'logs'. Update tests/test_cli.py:25-40 to insert 'session' between 'runtime' and 'logs' in the asserted ordered list. Make warm-managed default for `vibecomfy run` (vibecomfy/commands/run.py): change --runtime default from 'embedded' to 'auto'; at top of _cmd_run BEFORE get_schema_provider, when args.runtime in ('auto','server') and --server-url not set, call find_active_session('default'); resolve effective runtime (auto + warm \u2192 server@URL, auto + no warm \u2192 embedded one-shot, server + warm \u2192 reuse, server + no warm \u2192 existing one-shot managed). Pass URL into both get_schema_provider('auto', server_url=session_url) AND run_sync(..., server_url=session_url, ...).",

Critical correctness traps to remember while executing: (1) The _partition_comfy_config dual-vocabulary contract is the single most-flagged correctness item across five gate iterations \u2014 both typed SessionConfig field names AND raw HiddenSwitch keys must be accepted in one dict; the typed-vs-HS conflict test (T4 case 8) and the daemon carry-through test (T7) are the canonical assertions. Don't change the public signature. (3) Helper relocation is one-way (run.py imports session.py, never the reverse). Anything containing 'Loader' (and not in OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE) qualifies. Edge references (list values) are excluded \u2014 string values only. (5) Warm-default routing: --runtime default flips from 'embedded' to 'auto'. The schema-provider URL must be threaded BEFORE get_schema_provider construction so a second cold runtime doesn't spawn for /object_info. Both run_sync AND get_schema_provider receive the same URL. (6) test_cli.py:25-40 has an exact-ordered COMMANDS assertion \u2014 it WILL break when 'session' is registered unless updated in the same change. (7) /api/free is queue-async; tests assert payload, not synchronous unload completion. (8) Daemon spawn uses start_new_session=True; pidfile + URL files under out/sessions/<id>/. find_active_session cleans up stale pidfiles when os.kill(pid, 0) raises. (9) GPU smoke (T9) is info-only; the dev mac can't run it. The test layer (spawn count == 1 + /api/free payload) is the real warmth-verification gate. - [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
vibecomfy: warm-runtime sessions (keep models resident across generations by reusing Comfy's existing model_management, not reinventing it).

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy
Embedded ComfyUI fork in use: hiddenswitch (path: /Users/user_c042661f/.cache/uv/git-v0/checkouts/b790d0c34622492a/c5ed9402/)

# Problem
Today both vibecomfy runtime paths are cold-per-call:
- Embedded: vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125-138 enters/exits a fresh Comfy() context per run. The exit calls embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup -> model_management.unload_all_models() + gc + soft_empty_cache. Models are dumped after every generation.
- Managed server: vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:16 starts comfyui as a subprocess, queues once, terminates. Same outcome at the process level.

ComfyUI itself already keeps models warm by default — current_loaded_models persists in process, load_models_gpu reuses resident copies, eviction is pressure-driven, OOM is auto-handled (execution.py:735-737), and DISABLE_SMART_MEMORY is the only thing that forces unload after each prompt. So the work is structural: stop tearing the runtime down.

# Goal
Add session-aware warm runtime to vibecomfy with two backends sharing one abstraction:
1. Long-lived managed Comfy server (HTTP control plane) — default for CLI users.
2. Long-lived embedded Comfy() context — for in-process callers (runpod worker, tests, tight matrix loops).

Default policy: keep warm. Explicit flush available. Auto-flush on detected model-set change under VRAM pressure.

# Verified facts (don't re-research)
- POST /free and POST /api/free both exist in the embedded hiddenswitch fork at comfy/cmd/server.py:1041 with /api mirror auto-added at :1357. Payload: {"unload_models": bool, "free_memory": bool}. They set queue flags via prompt_queue.set_flag(...) — async, take effect at next prompt boundary, not synchronously.
- CLI flags exist in comfy/cli_args.py:115-160: --highvram, --lowvram, --normalvram, --reserve-vram <GB>, --disable-smart-memory, --cache-classic, --cache-lru N, --cache-none.
- Comfy.clear_cache() (embedded_comfy_client.py:385) and Comfy.reconfigure() (:398) already exposed; reconfigure uses MODEL_MANAGEMENT_ARGS fingerprint to avoid unnecessary subprocess cycling.
- The cleanup that nukes warm state lives in embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup (lines ~149-169) and is called from Comfy.__aexit__ (line ~481).

# Design constraints / refinements over the user's notes
- Do NOT default to --highvram (breaks multi-model workflows that need offload). Default to Comfy's smart memory.
- /free is asynchronous — flush API must either drain via a no-op prompt or document the queue-flag semantics. Tests must account for this.
- Auto-flush should only trigger on model-set fingerprint change AND VRAM-headroom-below-threshold. Input/seed/prompt changes must NOT trigger flush.
- VIBECOMFY_WARM=auto|always|never controls policy (auto default).
- Session lifecycle: daemon-style for CLI (pidfile + URL file under out/sessions/<id>/) so multiple `vibecomfy run` invocations share one warm pool. Embedded session is just a Python context manager.
- Browser-only tasks: there are none in this plan (no UI work).
- No hard-halt prereq clauses: each milestone is independently shippable.

# Out of scope for this megaplan
- Multi-GPU session pooling.
- Distributed/remote session sharing across machines.
- Workflow-graph-level caching (Comfy already has --cache-lru / --cache-classic).
- Replacing the existing one-shot run/run_embedded APIs — they should remain as thin wrappers around the new session.
- Cloud/RunPod orchestration changes (the runpod worker can adopt EmbeddedSession in a separate plan).

# Suggested milestone shape (megaplan can refine during prep/plan)
M1: Session abstraction + embedded backend
  - vibecomfy/runtime/session.py: VibeSession protocol + EmbeddedSession holding a long-lived Comfy() across multiple .run(workflow) calls.
  - .flush() -> Comfy.clear_cache(); .reconfigure(config) -> Comfy.reconfigure().
  - Refactor run_embedded() to be a one-shot wrapper around EmbeddedSession.
  - Tests: two sequential runs in one session reuse models (assert via inspecting comfy.model_management.current_loaded_models after each run, or via a stub model manager); flush() drops them; reconfigure with same fingerprint is a no-op.

M2: Managed server backend
  - ServerSession: keeps comfy_server subprocess alive across .run() calls. Start = spawn + wait-ready. .flush() = POST /api/free with appropriate flags. .stop() = SIGTERM with timeout fallback to SIGKILL.
  - Daemon mode: `vibecomfy session start [--id NAME] [--vram-policy ...]` writes pidfile + URL to out/sessions/<id>/; `vibecomfy session stop <id>`; `vibecomfy session list`; `vibecomfy session flush <id>`.
  - `vibecomfy run` auto-discovers active session if one exists, else falls back to one-shot managed mode.
  - Tests: session start/stop, flush via HTTP, two runs share warmth, daemon survives across CLI invocations.

M3: Auto-flush policy + fingerprinting
  - vibecomfy/runtime/model_fingerprint.py: extract loader-node identity tuples from a workflow (CheckpointLoaderSimple.ckpt_name, LoraLoader.lora_name, UNETLoader.unet_name, VAELoader.vae_name, CLIPLoader.clip_name, DualCLIPLoader, etc. — discover the canonical set from the embedded fork's nodes).
  - Session.run() compares current workflow fingerprint to last-run fingerprint. If different AND free VRAM < threshold (configurable, default e.g. 2GB), call flush() before queueing.
  - VIBECOMFY_WARM env var: auto (default) | always | never.
  - Tests: same model + different seed -> no flush; different ckpt -> flush; auto-flush gated by VRAM headroom (mock get_free_memory).

M4: VRAM/cache flag plumbing + docs
  - Session config accepts: vram_policy ("auto"|"high"|"low"|"normal"), reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy ("smart"|"classic"|"lru:N"|"none"), disable_smart_memory.
  - Threaded through to embedded Configuration AND server CLI args.
  - docs/runtime_lifecycle.md: add "Warm sessions" section. docs/runtime_surface.md: document VibeSession API.
  - Smoke test: `vibecomfy session start && vibecomfy run x.json && vibecomfy run y.json && vibecomfy session stop` — second run is materially faster than first (timing-based, with tolerance).

# Open questions for the planning step
- Should `vibecomfy run` lazily start a session on first call and reuse it (implicit warm) vs always require `session start`? Lean toward explicit start for predictability.
- Does the runpod worker (reigh-workspace/reigh-worker) want to consume EmbeddedSession directly or talk to a local managed session? Probably embedded — it owns the process lifecycle already. Out of scope for this plan, but the API should not preclude it.
- Do we need partial flush (just CLIP, just VAE, just UNet)? Probably no — Comfy's pressure-driven eviction handles per-model decisions; only expose all-or-nothing flush at the session boundary.

# Success criteria (suggested — planning phase should refine)
- A `VibeSession` abstraction exists with both Embedded and Server backends.
- Two sequential `.run()` calls in one session share warm models (verified by test, not just timing).
- `flush()` is explicit and works for both backends. For the server backend, the test accounts for /free's queue-flag semantics.
- `vibecomfy session start/stop/list/flush` CLI commands exist and survive across separate `vibecomfy` invocations.
- Auto-flush policy fires on model-set change under VRAM pressure, never on seed/input changes.
- Existing `run` and `run_embedded` one-shot APIs continue to work unchanged (backwards compatibility).
- Documentation updated in docs/runtime_lifecycle.md and docs/runtime_surface.md.

Treat raw HS keys (reserve_vram, cache_none, --highvram, etc.) via the existing HiddenSwitch translation path. Unknown keys go to extra. Tests in Step 7.3 expect typed-name daemon config carry-through; tests for the embedded path may also feed raw HS keys.

Daemon body: `config_dict = json.loads(args.config); session = ServerSession(SessionConfig.from_dict(config_dict)); await session.start(); write out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}; install SIGTERM handler that calls await session.stop() and removes pidfile; sleep on asyncio.Event`. Daemonize from `session start` via `subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-m', 'vibecomfy.commands.session', '--daemon', '--id', id_, '--config', json.dumps(config)], stdout=DEVNULL, stderr=open(log_path,'ab'), start_new_session=True)`. Parent assembles config dict from CLI flags as TYPED-NAME dict matching SessionConfig field names (omit None values): `{port, vram_policy, reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy, warm_policy, disable_smart_memory}`. Critical correctness traps to remember while executing: (1) The _partition_comfy_config dual-vocabulary contract is the single most-flagged correctness item across five gate iterations \u2014 both typed SessionConfig field names AND raw HiddenSwitch keys must be accepted in one dict; the typed-vs-HS conflict test (T4 case 8) and the daemon carry-through test (T7) are the canonical assertions. Get this wrong and the FLAG-012 work was wasted. (2) _embedded_configuration MUST keep its `(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> Configuration` signature unchanged. The new logic lives in _embedded_configuration_for_session(SessionConfig); the workflow-taking helper is a one-line wrapper that routes through SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata. Don't change the public signature. (3) Helper relocation is one-way (run.py imports session.py, never the reverse). The circular import was specifically called out and resolved \u2014 don't reintroduce it. (4) Pattern-based fingerprint, NOT a hard-coded class list. Anything containing 'Loader' (and not in OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE) qualifies. Edge references (list values) are excluded \u2014 string values only. (5) Warm-default routing: --runtime default flips from 'embedded' to 'auto'. The schema-provider URL must be threaded BEFORE get_schema_provider construction so a second cold runtime doesn't spawn for /object_info. Both run_sync AND get_schema_provider receive the same URL. (6) test_cli.py:25-40 has an exact-ordered COMMANDS assertion \u2014 it WILL break when 'session' is registered unless updated in the same change. (7) /api/free is queue-async; tests assert payload, not synchronous unload completion. (8) Daemon spawn uses start_new_session=True; pidfile + URL files under out/sessions/<id>/. find_active_session cleans up stale pidfiles when os.kill(pid, 0) raises. (9) GPU smoke (T9) is info-only; the dev mac can't run it. The test layer (spawn count == 1 + /api/free payload) is the real warmth-verification gate. - [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. - [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.   "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/vibecomfy/runtime/session.py\n@@\n class ServerSession:\n@@\n         self.url = None\n         self.log_handle = None\n \n \n+def find_active_session(id: str = \"default\") -> str | None:\n+    session_dir = Path(\"out/sessions\") / id\n+    pid_path = session_dir / \"pid\"\n+    url_path = session_dir / \"url\"\n+    if not pid_path.exists() or not url_path.exists():\n+        _cleanup_session_files(session_dir)\n+        return None\n+    try:\n+        pid = int(pid_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip())\n+        url = url_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n+    except (OSError, ValueError):\n+        _cleanup_session_files(session_dir)\n+        return None\n+    if not url:\n+        _cleanup_session_files(session_dir)\n+        return None\n+    try:\n+        os.kill(pid, 0)\n+    except ProcessLookupError:\n+        _cleanup_session_files(session_dir)\n+        return None\n+    except PermissionError:\n+        return url\n+    except OSError:\n+        _cleanup_session_files(session_dir)\n+        return None\n+    return url\n+\n+\n+def _cleanup_session_files(session_dir: Path) -> None:\n+    for name in (\"pid\", \"url\", \"config.json\"):\n+        try:\n+            (session_dir / name).unlink()\n+        except FileNotFoundError:\n+            pass\n+\n+\n def _partition_comfy_config(values: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:\n*** End Patch\n"
}

session.run(_workflow(\"model-b.safetensors\"))\n+        finally:\n+            await session.stop()\n+\n+    asyncio.run(run_cases())\n+\n+    assert fake_comfy.instances[0].clear_cache_calls == 0\n*** End Patch\n"

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
vibecomfy: warm-runtime sessions (keep models resident across generations by reusing Comfy's existing model_management, not reinventing it).

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy
Embedded ComfyUI fork in use: hiddenswitch (path: /Users/user_c042661f/.cache/uv/git-v0/checkouts/b790d0c34622492a/c5ed9402/)

# Problem
Today both vibecomfy runtime paths are cold-per-call:
- Embedded: vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125-138 enters/exits a fresh Comfy() context per run. The exit calls embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup -> model_management.unload_all_models() + gc + soft_empty_cache. Models are dumped after every generation.
- Managed server: vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:16 starts comfyui as a subprocess, queues once, terminates. Same outcome at the process level.

ComfyUI itself already keeps models warm by default — current_loaded_models persists in process, load_models_gpu reuses resident copies, eviction is pressure-driven, OOM is auto-handled (execution.py:735-737), and DISABLE_SMART_MEMORY is the only thing that forces unload after each prompt. So the work is structural: stop tearing the runtime down.

# Goal
Add session-aware warm runtime to vibecomfy with two backends sharing one abstraction:
1. Long-lived managed Comfy server (HTTP control plane) — default for CLI users.
2. Long-lived embedded Comfy() context — for in-process callers (runpod worker, tests, tight matrix loops).

Default policy: keep warm. Explicit flush available. Auto-flush on detected model-set change under VRAM pressure.

# Verified facts (don't re-research)
- POST /free and POST /api/free both exist in the embedded hiddenswitch fork at comfy/cmd/server.py:1041 with /api mirror auto-added at :1357. Payload: {"unload_models": bool, "free_memory": bool}. They set queue flags via prompt_queue.set_flag(...) — async, take effect at next prompt boundary, not synchronously.
- CLI flags exist in comfy/cli_args.py:115-160: --highvram, --lowvram, --normalvram, --reserve-vram <GB>, --disable-smart-memory, --cache-classic, --cache-lru N, --cache-none.
- Comfy.clear_cache() (embedded_comfy_client.py:385) and Comfy.reconfigure() (:398) already exposed; reconfigure uses MODEL_MANAGEMENT_ARGS fingerprint to avoid unnecessary subprocess cycling.
- The cleanup that nukes warm state lives in embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup (lines ~149-169) and is called from Comfy.__aexit__ (line ~481).

# Design constraints / refinements over the user's notes
- Do NOT default to --highvram (breaks multi-model workflows that need offload). Default to Comfy's smart memory.
- /free is asynchronous — flush API must either drain via a no-op prompt or document the queue-flag semantics. Tests must account for this.
- Auto-flush should only trigger on model-set fingerprint change AND VRAM-headroom-below-threshold. Input/seed/prompt changes must NOT trigger flush.
- VIBECOMFY_WARM=auto|always|never controls policy (auto default).
- Session lifecycle: daemon-style for CLI (pidfile + URL file under out/sessions/<id>/) so multiple `vibecomfy run` invocations share one warm pool. Embedded session is just a Python context manager.
- Browser-only tasks: there are none in this plan (no UI work).
- No hard-halt prereq clauses: each milestone is independently shippable.

# Out of scope for this megaplan
- Multi-GPU session pooling.
- Distributed/remote session sharing across machines.
- Workflow-graph-level caching (Comfy already has --cache-lru / --cache-classic).
- Replacing the existing one-shot run/run_embedded APIs — they should remain as thin wrappers around the new session.
- Cloud/RunPod orchestration changes (the runpod worker can adopt EmbeddedSession in a separate plan).

# Suggested milestone shape (megaplan can refine during prep/plan)
M1: Session abstraction + embedded backend
  - vibecomfy/runtime/session.py: VibeSession protocol + EmbeddedSession holding a long-lived Comfy() across multiple .run(workflow) calls.
  - .flush() -> Comfy.clear_cache(); .reconfigure(config) -> Comfy.reconfigure().
  - Refactor run_embedded() to be a one-shot wrapper around EmbeddedSession.
  - Tests: two sequential runs in one session reuse models (assert via inspecting comfy.model_management.current_loaded_models after each run, or via a stub model manager); flush() drops them; reconfigure with same fingerprint is a no-op.

M2: Managed server backend
  - ServerSession: keeps comfy_server subprocess alive across .run() calls. Start = spawn + wait-ready. .flush() = POST /api/free with appropriate flags. .stop() = SIGTERM with timeout fallback to SIGKILL.
  - Daemon mode: `vibecomfy session start [--id NAME] [--vram-policy ...]` writes pidfile + URL to out/sessions/<id>/; `vibecomfy session stop <id>`; `vibecomfy session list`; `vibecomfy session flush <id>`.
  - `vibecomfy run` auto-discovers active session if one exists, else falls back to one-shot managed mode.
  - Tests: session start/stop, flush via HTTP, two runs share warmth, daemon survives across CLI invocations.

M3: Auto-flush policy + fingerprinting
  - vibecomfy/runtime/model_fingerprint.py: extract loader-node identity tuples from a workflow (CheckpointLoaderSimple.ckpt_name, LoraLoader.lora_name, UNETLoader.unet_name, VAELoader.vae_name, CLIPLoader.clip_name, DualCLIPLoader, etc. — discover the canonical set from the embedded fork's nodes).
  - Session.run() compares current workflow fingerprint to last-run fingerprint. If different AND free VRAM < threshold (configurable, default e.g. 2GB), call flush() before queueing.
  - VIBECOMFY_WARM env var: auto (default) | always | never.
  - Tests: same model + different seed -> no flush; different ckpt -> flush; auto-flush gated by VRAM headroom (mock get_free_memory).

M4: VRAM/cache flag plumbing + docs
  - Session config accepts: vram_policy ("auto"|"high"|"low"|"normal"), reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy ("smart"|"classic"|"lru:N"|"none"), disable_smart_memory.
  - Threaded through to embedded Configuration AND server CLI args.
  - docs/runtime_lifecycle.md: add "Warm sessions" section. docs/runtime_surface.md: document VibeSession API.
  - Smoke test: `vibecomfy session start && vibecomfy run x.json && vibecomfy run y.json && vibecomfy session stop` — second run is materially faster than first (timing-based, with tolerance).

# Open questions for the planning step
- Should `vibecomfy run` lazily start a session on first call and reuse it (implicit warm) vs always require `session start`? Lean toward explicit start for predictability.
- Does the runpod worker (reigh-workspace/reigh-worker) want to consume EmbeddedSession directly or talk to a local managed session? Probably embedded — it owns the process lifecycle already. Out of scope for this plan, but the API should not preclude it.
- Do we need partial flush (just CLIP, just VAE, just UNet)? Probably no — Comfy's pressure-driven eviction handles per-model decisions; only expose all-or-nothing flush at the session boundary.

# Success criteria (suggested — planning phase should refine)
- A `VibeSession` abstraction exists with both Embedded and Server backends.
- Two sequential `.run()` calls in one session share warm models (verified by test, not just timing).
- `flush()` is explicit and works for both backends. For the server backend, the test accounts for /free's queue-flag semantics.
- `vibecomfy session start/stop/list/flush` CLI commands exist and survive across separate `vibecomfy` invocations.
- Auto-flush policy fires on model-set change under VRAM pressure, never on seed/input changes.
- Existing `run` and `run_embedded` one-shot APIs continue to work unchanged (backwards compatibility).
- Documentation updated in docs/runtime_lifecycle.md and docs/runtime_surface.md.

User notes and answers:

Recommended execution order matches plan validation order: Phase 1 first (T1\u2192T2\u2192T3\u2192T4), then Phase 2 (T5\u2192T6\u2192T7), then docs (T8) and smoke (T9), then T10 to validate. Critical correctness traps to remember while executing: (1) The _partition_comfy_config dual-vocabulary contract is the single most-flagged correctness item across five gate iterations \u2014 both typed SessionConfig field names AND raw HiddenSwitch keys must be accepted in one dict; the typed-vs-HS conflict test (T4 case 8) and the daemon carry-through test (T7) are the canonical assertions. Get this wrong and the FLAG-012 work was wasted. (2) _embedded_configuration MUST keep its `(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> Configuration` signature unchanged. The new logic lives in _embedded_configuration_for_session(SessionConfig); the workflow-taking helper is a one-line wrapper that routes through SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata. Don't change the public signature. (3) Helper relocation is one-way (run.py imports session.py, never the reverse). The circular import was specifically called out and resolved \u2014 don't reintroduce it. (4) Pattern-based fingerprint, NOT a hard-coded class list. Anything containing 'Loader' (and not in OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE) qualifies. Edge references (list values) are excluded \u2014 string values only. (5) Warm-default routing: --runtime default flips from 'embedded' to 'auto'. The schema-provider URL must be threaded BEFORE get_schema_provider construction so a second cold runtime doesn't spawn for /object_info. Both run_sync AND get_schema_provider receive the same URL. (6) test_cli.py:25-40 has an exact-ordered COMMANDS assertion \u2014 it WILL break when 'session' is registered unless updated in the same change. (7) /api/free is queue-async; tests assert payload, not synchronous unload completion. (8) Daemon spawn uses start_new_session=True; pidfile + URL files under out/sessions/<id>/. find_active_session cleans up stale pidfiles when os.kill(pid, 0) raises. (9) GPU smoke (T9) is info-only; the dev mac can't run it. The test layer (spawn count == 1 + /api/free payload) is the real warmth-verification gate. Read tests/test_runtime_run.py:140-149 first to copy the sys.modules injection pattern \u2014 all new tests use it.         Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy

        Idea:
vibecomfy: warm-runtime sessions (keep models resident across generations by reusing Comfy's existing model_management, not reinventing it).

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy
Embedded ComfyUI fork in use: hiddenswitch (path: /Users/user_c042661f/.cache/uv/git-v0/checkouts/b790d0c34622492a/c5ed9402/)

# Problem
Today both vibecomfy runtime paths are cold-per-call:
- Embedded: vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125-138 enters/exits a fresh Comfy() context per run. The exit calls embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup -> model_management.unload_all_models() + gc + soft_empty_cache. Models are dumped after every generation.
- Managed server: vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:16 starts comfyui as a subprocess, queues once, terminates. Same outcome at the process level.

ComfyUI itself already keeps models warm by default — current_loaded_models persists in process, load_models_gpu reuses resident copies, eviction is pressure-driven, OOM is auto-handled (execution.py:735-737), and DISABLE_SMART_MEMORY is the only thing that forces unload after each prompt. So the work is structural: stop tearing the runtime down.

# Goal
Add session-aware warm runtime to vibecomfy with two backends sharing one abstraction:
1. Long-lived managed Comfy server (HTTP control plane) — default for CLI users.
2. Long-lived embedded Comfy() context — for in-process callers (runpod worker, tests, tight matrix loops).

Default policy: keep warm. Explicit flush available. Auto-flush on detected model-set change under VRAM pressure.

# Verified facts (don't re-research)
- POST /free and POST /api/free both exist in the embedded hiddenswitch fork at comfy/cmd/server.py:1041 with /api mirror auto-added at :1357. Payload: {"unload_models": bool, "free_memory": bool}. They set queue flags via prompt_queue.set_flag(...) — async, take effect at next prompt boundary, not synchronously.
- CLI flags exist in comfy/cli_args.py:115-160: --highvram, --lowvram, --normalvram, --reserve-vram <GB>, --disable-smart-memory, --cache-classic, --cache-lru N, --cache-none.
- Comfy.clear_cache() (embedded_comfy_client.py:385) and Comfy.reconfigure() (:398) already exposed; reconfigure uses MODEL_MANAGEMENT_ARGS fingerprint to avoid unnecessary subprocess cycling.
- The cleanup that nukes warm state lives in embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup (lines ~149-169) and is called from Comfy.__aexit__ (line ~481).

# Design constraints / refinements over the user's notes
- Do NOT default to --highvram (breaks multi-model workflows that need offload). Default to Comfy's smart memory.
- /free is asynchronous — flush API must either drain via a no-op prompt or document the queue-flag semantics. Tests must account for this.
- Auto-flush should only trigger on model-set fingerprint change AND VRAM-headroom-below-threshold. Input/seed/prompt changes must NOT trigger flush.
- VIBECOMFY_WARM=auto|always|never controls policy (auto default).
- Session lifecycle: daemon-style for CLI (pidfile + URL file under out/sessions/<id>/) so multiple `vibecomfy run` invocations share one warm pool. Embedded session is just a Python context manager.
- Browser-only tasks: there are none in this plan (no UI work).
- No hard-halt prereq clauses: each milestone is independently shippable.

# Out of scope for this megaplan
- Multi-GPU session pooling.
- Distributed/remote session sharing across machines.
- Workflow-graph-level caching (Comfy already has --cache-lru / --cache-classic).
- Replacing the existing one-shot run/run_embedded APIs — they should remain as thin wrappers around the new session.
- Cloud/RunPod orchestration changes (the runpod worker can adopt EmbeddedSession in a separate plan).

# Suggested milestone shape (megaplan can refine during prep/plan)
M1: Session abstraction + embedded backend
  - vibecomfy/runtime/session.py: VibeSession protocol + EmbeddedSession holding a long-lived Comfy() across multiple .run(workflow) calls.
  - .flush() -> Comfy.clear_cache(); .reconfigure(config) -> Comfy.reconfigure().
  - Refactor run_embedded() to be a one-shot wrapper around EmbeddedSession.
  - Tests: two sequential runs in one session reuse models (assert via inspecting comfy.model_management.current_loaded_models after each run, or via a stub model manager); flush() drops them; reconfigure with same fingerprint is a no-op.

M2: Managed server backend
  - ServerSession: keeps comfy_server subprocess alive across .run() calls. Start = spawn + wait-ready. .flush() = POST /api/free with appropriate flags. .stop() = SIGTERM with timeout fallback to SIGKILL.
  - Daemon mode: `vibecomfy session start [--id NAME] [--vram-policy ...]` writes pidfile + URL to out/sessions/<id>/; `vibecomfy session stop <id>`; `vibecomfy session list`; `vibecomfy session flush <id>`.
  - `vibecomfy run` auto-discovers active session if one exists, else falls back to one-shot managed mode.
  - Tests: session start/stop, flush via HTTP, two runs share warmth, daemon survives across CLI invocations.

M3: Auto-flush policy + fingerprinting
  - vibecomfy/runtime/model_fingerprint.py: extract loader-node identity tuples from a workflow (CheckpointLoaderSimple.ckpt_name, LoraLoader.lora_name, UNETLoader.unet_name, VAELoader.vae_name, CLIPLoader.clip_name, DualCLIPLoader, etc. — discover the canonical set from the embedded fork's nodes).
  - Session.run() compares current workflow fingerprint to last-run fingerprint. If different AND free VRAM < threshold (configurable, default e.g. 2GB), call flush() before queueing.
  - VIBECOMFY_WARM env var: auto (default) | always | never.
  - Tests: same model + different seed -> no flush; different ckpt -> flush; auto-flush gated by VRAM headroom (mock get_free_memory).

M4: VRAM/cache flag plumbing + docs
  - Session config accepts: vram_policy ("auto"|"high"|"low"|"normal"), reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy ("smart"|"classic"|"lru:N"|"none"), disable_smart_memory.
  - Threaded through to embedded Configuration AND server CLI args.
  - docs/runtime_lifecycle.md: add "Warm sessions" section. docs/runtime_surface.md: document VibeSession API.
  - Smoke test: `vibecomfy session start && vibecomfy run x.json && vibecomfy run y.json && vibecomfy session stop` — second run is materially faster than first (timing-based, with tolerance).

# Open questions for the planning step
- Should `vibecomfy run` lazily start a session on first call and reuse it (implicit warm) vs always require `session start`? Lean toward explicit start for predictability.
- Does the runpod worker (reigh-workspace/reigh-worker) want to consume EmbeddedSession directly or talk to a local managed session? Probably embedded — it owns the process lifecycle already. Out of scope for this plan, but the API should not preclude it.
- Do we need partial flush (just CLIP, just VAE, just UNet)? Probably no — Comfy's pressure-driven eviction handles per-model decisions; only expose all-or-nothing flush at the session boundary.

# Success criteria (suggested — planning phase should refine)
- A `VibeSession` abstraction exists with both Embedded and Server backends.
- Two sequential `.run()` calls in one session share warm models (verified by test, not just timing).
- `flush()` is explicit and works for both backends. For the server backend, the test accounts for /free's queue-flag semantics.
- `vibecomfy session start/stop/list/flush` CLI commands exist and survive across separate `vibecomfy` invocations.
- Auto-flush policy fires on model-set change under VRAM pressure, never on seed/input changes.
- Existing `run` and `run_embedded` one-shot APIs continue to work unchanged (backwards compatibility).
- Documentation updated in docs/runtime_lifecycle.md and docs/runtime_surface.md.

User notes and answers:
- FLAG-012 fix: _partition_comfy_config must accept both typed SessionConfig field names and raw HiddenSwitch keys. Treat typed names (port, vram_policy, cache_policy, warm_policy, reserve_vram_gb) as direct routes to typed dataclass fields. Treat raw HS keys (reserve_vram, cache_none, --highvram, etc.) via the existing HiddenSwitch translation path. Unknown keys go to extra. Tests in Step 7.3 expect typed-name daemon config carry-through; tests for the embedded path may also feed raw HS keys.

(4) `from_workflow_metadata` carry-through (raw HS): metadata `{reserve_vram: 12, cache_none: True, fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc: True}` \u2192 config.reserve_vram_gb == 12, cache_policy == 'none', extra == {fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc: True}; then _embedded_configuration_for_session yields Configuration with all three keys. (5) `from_dict` mirror with same raw-HS dict produces identical SessionConfig. (6) `from_dict` typed names: `{port: 8200, vram_policy: 'high', reserve_vram_gb: 2.0, cache_policy: 'lru:3', warm_policy: 'always'}` \u2192 SessionConfig(port=8200, vram_policy='high', reserve_vram_gb=2.0, cache_policy='lru:3', warm_policy='always', extra={}). (7) `from_dict` mixed: `{port: 8200, reserve_vram: 12, fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc: True}` \u2192 SessionConfig(port=8200, reserve_vram_gb=12, extra={fp8_e4m3fn_text_enc: True}). (8) typed-vs-HS conflict: `{reserve_vram_gb: 2.0, reserve_vram: 12}` resolves to reserve_vram_gb == 2.0. (9) empty: `from_dict({})` \u2192 all defaults, empty extra. (10) Pattern-based fingerprint snapshot tests: `wan_t2v.api.json` (UNETLoader/CLIPLoader/VAELoader widget_0), `ltx2_3_t2v.api.json` (LowVRAMCheckpointLoader, LowVRAMAudioVAELoader, LTXAVTextEncoderLoader, LoraLoaderModelOnly), synthetic dict with DualCLIPLoaderGGUF/UnetLoaderGGUF \u2014 assert edge references (list values) NOT included. (11) Auto-flush truth table with mocked _free_vram_gb: same fingerprint + low VRAM \u2192 no flush; different fingerprint + low VRAM \u2192 flush; different fingerprint + high VRAM \u2192 no flush; same loader + different KSampler seed \u2192 no flush. (12) VIBECOMFY_WARM=never flushes before every run; VIBECOMFY_WARM=always never auto-flushes. Confirm existing tests/test_runtime_run.py:98-121 (test_embedded_configuration_uses_hiddenswitch_configuration_object) passes unchanged.",

Daemon invocation: `python -m vibecomfy.commands.session --daemon --id NAME --config <json>`. Daemon body: `config_dict = json.loads(args.config); session = ServerSession(SessionConfig.from_dict(config_dict)); await session.start(); write out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}; install SIGTERM handler that calls await session.stop() and removes pidfile; sleep on asyncio.Event`. Daemonize from `session start` via `subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-m', 'vibecomfy.commands.session', '--daemon', '--id', id_, '--config', json.dumps(config)], stdout=DEVNULL, stderr=open(log_path,'ab'), start_new_session=True)`. Parent assembles config dict from CLI flags as TYPED-NAME dict matching SessionConfig field names (omit None values): `{port, vram_policy, reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy, warm_policy, disable_smart_memory}`. Parent waits up to 120 s for out/sessions/<id>/url. Subcommands: `start [--id NAME=default] [--vram-policy auto|high|low|normal] [--reserve-vram-gb F] [--cache-policy smart|classic|lru:N|none] [--disable-smart-memory] [--warm-policy auto|always|never] [--port P=8188]`, `stop <id>`, `list`, `flush <id> [--unload-models/--no-unload-models] [--free-memory/--no-free-memory]`, `status <id>`. Update tests/test_cli.py:25-40 to insert 'session' between 'runtime' and 'logs' in the asserted ordered list.       "id": "T6",
      "description": "Create vibecomfy/commands/session.py with argparse for start/stop/list/flush/status AND in-process daemon entrypoint (single file). Daemon invocation: `python -m vibecomfy.commands.session --daemon --id NAME --config <json>`. Daemon body: `config_dict = json.loads(args.config); session = ServerSession(SessionConfig.from_dict(config_dict)); await session.start(); write out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}; install SIGTERM handler that calls await session.stop() and removes pidfile; sleep on asyncio.Event`. Daemonize from `session start` via `subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-m', 'vibecomfy.commands.session', '--daemon', '--id', id_, '--config', json.dumps(config)], stdout=DEVNULL, stderr=open(log_path,'ab'), start_new_session=True)`. Parent assembles config dict from CLI flags as TYPED-NAME dict matching SessionConfig field names (omit None values): `{port, vram_policy, reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy, warm_policy, disable_smart_memory}`. Parent waits up to 120 s for out/sessions/<id>/url. Subcommands: `start [--id NAME=default] [--vram-policy auto|high|low|normal] [--reserve-vram-gb F] [--cache-policy smart|classic|lru:N|none] [--disable-smart-memory] [--warm-policy auto|always|never] [--port P=8188]`, `stop <id>`, `list`, `flush <id> [--unload-models/--no-unload-models] [--free-memory/--no-free-memory]`, `status <id>`. Add `find_active_session(id='default') -> str | None` in runtime/session.py: read out/sessions/<id>/url; if pidfile present and os.kill(pid, 0) succeeds return URL else clean up and return None. Register CommandSpec('session', 'vibecomfy.commands.session') in vibecomfy/commands/__init__.py:14-28 BETWEEN 'runtime' and 'logs'. Update tests/test_cli.py:25-40 to insert 'session' between 'runtime' and 'logs' in the asserted ordered list. Make warm-managed default for `vibecomfy run` (vibecomfy/commands/run.py): change --runtime default from 'embedded' to 'auto'; at top of _cmd_run BEFORE get_schema_provider, when args.runtime in ('auto','server') and --server-url not set, call find_active_session('default'); resolve effective runtime (auto + warm \u2192 server@URL, auto + no warm \u2192 embedded one-shot, server + warm \u2192 reuse, server + no warm \u2192 existing one-shot managed). Pass URL into both get_schema_provider('auto', server_url=session_url) AND run_sync(..., server_url=session_url, ...).",

    "FLAG-012 (must): _partition_comfy_config MUST accept BOTH typed SessionConfig field names AND raw HiddenSwitch keys in the same dict. Conflict precedence: HS-translated keys applied first, typed-name keys overwrite. The typed-vs-HS conflict test (T4 case 8) is the canonical assertion.",
    "FLAG-010/callers (must): _embedded_configuration KEEPS its existing `(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> Configuration` public signature. Three current callers \u2014 vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125, tests/test_runtime_run.py:116, vibecomfy/commands/doctor.py:56-60 \u2014 MUST work without modification. The actual logic moves to _embedded_configuration_for_session(config: SessionConfig); the workflow-taking helper is a one-line wrapper. run.py re-exports it so `from vibecomfy.runtime.run import _embedded_configuration` keeps resolving.",
    "FLAG-007/circular import: relocation direction is one-way \u2014 run.py imports from session.py, never the reverse. Verify after T1 with `python -c 'import vibecomfy.runtime.session; import vibecomfy.runtime.run'`.",
    "FLAG-006 fingerprint scope: the extractor is PATTERN-BASED ('Loader' substring), NOT a hard-coded class list. Empty OVERRIDES_INCLUDE/OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE sets reserved for future exceptions. The synthetic-dict + ltx2_3_t2v.api.json snapshot tests in T4 are the proof.",
    "Edge references (list values like `['12', 0]`) on a loader node must NOT be included in the fingerprint \u2014 only non-edge string-valued slots. Test must assert this explicitly.",
    "FLAG-008/test_cli.py registry: when adding `CommandSpec('session', ...)` between 'runtime' and 'logs', the existing exact-ordered COMMANDS assertion at tests/test_cli.py:25-40 will break unless updated in the same diff. T6 includes this update.",
    "FLAG-002 warm-default: --runtime default flips from 'embedded' to 'auto'. With no warm session present, behavior MUST remain backwards-compatible (embedded one-shot). The fallback regression test in T7 covers this.",
    "FLAG-004 schema-provider URL plumbing: get_schema_provider construction in _cmd_run must happen AFTER find_active_session and receive the discovered URL via server_url=session_url. Otherwise a second cold runtime would spawn for /object_info \u2014 the bug the brief explicitly calls out.",
    "/api/free is QUEUE-ASYNC (sets prompt_queue.set_flag), NOT synchronous unload. Tests assert payload + endpoint, not actual model-unload completion. ComfyClient.free docstring must call this out.",
    "stop() must tolerate `model_mmap_residency` AttributeError on Comfy.__aexit__ (this is the cleanup path that nukes warm state at embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup; tolerate it on shutdown).",
    "_free_vram_gb() must not crash on missing torch/comfy import \u2014 return float('inf') so the auto-flush gate is conservative on dev mac.",
    "SessionConfig field-name vs raw-HS-key collision (e.g. caller passes both `reserve_vram_gb=2.0` and `reserve_vram=12` in one dict): apply HS-translated keys first, typed-name keys overwrite. Document in _partition_comfy_config docstring.",
    "Daemon spawn uses `start_new_session=True` so the daemon survives parent shell exit. Pidfile + URL file land under out/sessions/<id>/ with default id 'default'. Stale pidfile auto-cleanup when recorded pid is dead.",
    "Auto-flush threshold default = 2.0 GB free VRAM. ServerSession default port 8188 with --port override.",
    "find_active_session must clean up stale pidfile/url files when os.kill(pid, 0) raises ProcessLookupError, then return None \u2014 otherwise warm-default routing will keep trying to attach to dead daemons.",
    "Manual GPU smoke (T9) cannot run on dev mac \u2014 info-only criterion. Test-layer subprocess-spawn-count + /api/free payload assertions are the actual verification gate for managed-mode warmth.",

Recommended execution order matches plan validation order: Phase 1 first (T1\u2192T2\u2192T3\u2192T4), then Phase 2 (T5\u2192T6\u2192T7), then docs (T8) and smoke (T9), then T10 to validate. Critical correctness traps to remember while executing: (1) The _partition_comfy_config dual-vocabulary contract is the single most-flagged correctness item across five gate iterations \u2014 both typed SessionConfig field names AND raw HiddenSwitch keys must be accepted in one dict; the typed-vs-HS conflict test (T4 case 8) and the daemon carry-through test (T7) are the canonical assertions. Get this wrong and the FLAG-012 work was wasted. (2) _embedded_configuration MUST keep its `(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> Configuration` signature unchanged. The new logic lives in _embedded_configuration_for_session(SessionConfig); the workflow-taking helper is a one-line wrapper that routes through SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata. Don't change the public signature. (3) Helper relocation is one-way (run.py imports session.py, never the reverse). The circular import was specifically called out and resolved \u2014 don't reintroduce it. (4) Pattern-based fingerprint, NOT a hard-coded class list. Anything containing 'Loader' (and not in OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE) qualifies. Edge references (list values) are excluded \u2014 string values only. (5) Warm-default routing: --runtime default flips from 'embedded' to 'auto'. The schema-provider URL must be threaded BEFORE get_schema_provider construction so a second cold runtime doesn't spawn for /object_info. Both run_sync AND get_schema_provider receive the same URL. (6) test_cli.py:25-40 has an exact-ordered COMMANDS assertion \u2014 it WILL break when 'session' is registered unless updated in the same change. (7) /api/free is queue-async; tests assert payload, not synchronous unload completion. (8) Daemon spawn uses start_new_session=True; pidfile + URL files under out/sessions/<id>/. find_active_session cleans up stale pidfiles when os.kill(pid, 0) raises. (9) GPU smoke (T9) is info-only; the dev mac can't run it. The test layer (spawn count == 1 + /api/free payload) is the real warmth-verification gate. Read tests/test_runtime_run.py:140-149 first to copy the sys.modules injection pattern \u2014 all new tests use it. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy/docs/runtime_lifecycle.md\n@@\n-CLI `vibecomfy run` defaults to embedded mode because HiddenSwitch's embedded client waits for workflow completion and returns output metadata. This is the strongest local end-to-end path.\n+CLI `vibecomfy run` defaults to `auto`: it reuses an active warm managed session when one is running, otherwise it falls back to embedded one-shot execution.\n@@\n The managed smoke started Comfy, read 1,202 node definitions from `/object_info`, and terminated. The embedded smoke workflows wrote PNG files under `output/`.\n+\n+## Warm Sessions\n+\n+VibeComfy has two warm-session backends:\n+\n+- `EmbeddedSession` keeps one HiddenSwitch `Comfy()` context open inside the current Python process. This is the backend for in-process callers that own the lifecycle, such as tests, tight local loops, and future worker integrations.\n+- `ServerSession` keeps one `comfyui serve` subprocess alive and talks to it over HTTP. This is the CLI-friendly backend because separate `vibecomfy run` invocations can share the same server process through files under `out/sessions/<id>/`.\n+\n+The session CLI manages daemon-style server sessions:\n+\n+```bash\n+vibecomfy session start --id default --port 8188\n+vibecomfy session status default\n+vibecomfy session list\n+vibecomfy session flush default\n+vibecomfy session stop default\n+```\n+\n+`session start` writes `pid`, `url`, and `config.json` under `out/sessions/<id>/`. `vibecomfy run --runtime auto` checks the default session before loading schemas or queueing work; if the session is alive it passes the same URL to schema discovery and execution. If no warm session is alive, `auto` uses embedded one-shot execution. `--runtime server` also reuses the active session when present, and otherwise keeps the existing one-shot managed-server behavior.\n+\n+Warm policy is controlled by `SessionConfig.warm_policy` and can be overridden with `VIBECOMFY_WARM`:\n+\n+- `auto` keeps models warm by default, but flushes before the next prompt when the workflow's model fingerprint changes and free VRAM is below the configured threshold.\n+- `always` keeps the resident models and disables the automatic pre-run flush.\n+- `never` flushes before every run.\n+\n+The auto-flush fingerprint is pattern-based. Any node whose `class_type` contains `Loader` contributes its non-edge string-valued input slots to the fingerprint, except for future explicit exclusions. Edge references such as `[\"12\", 0]`, seeds, prompts, and other non-loader inputs do not contribute, so seed or prompt changes do not trigger a flush.\n+\n+`session flush` and `ServerSession.flush()` call `POST /api/free` with `{\"unload_models\": true, \"free_memory\": true}`. In HiddenSwitch this endpoint sets queue flags; it is asynchronous and takes effect at the next prompt boundary rather than synchronously unloading models before the HTTP response returns. Embedded sessions call `Comfy.clear_cache()`.\n+\n+Session configuration covers the model-memory and cache flags that Comfy already exposes:\n+\n+- `vram_policy`: `auto`, `high`, `low`, or `normal`\n+- `reserve_vram_gb`\n+- `cache_policy`: `smart`, `classic`, `lru:N`, or `none`\n+- `disable_smart_memory`\n+- `port`\n+- `warm_policy`\n+\n+`SessionConfig.extra` is an escape hatch for raw HiddenSwitch configuration keys that VibeComfy does not type yet. Mixed dictionaries may include both typed field names and raw HiddenSwitch keys; raw keys are translated first, then typed field names win on conflicts.\n*** End Patch\n"
}

Server flushes call `/api/free`, which is queue-async and applies at the next prompt boundary.\n+- `reconfigure(config)` applies a new `SessionConfig`; embedded sessions pass it through to `Comfy.reconfigure()`, and server sessions restart only when the resulting Comfy CLI arguments change.\n+- `stop()` closes the embedded context or terminates the managed server process.\n+\n+`SessionConfig` fields:\n+\n+- `vram_policy`: `auto`, `high`, `low`, or `normal`\n+- `reserve_vram_gb`\n+- `cache_policy`: `smart`, `classic`, `lru:N`, or `none`\n+- `disable_smart_memory`\n+- `warm_policy`: `auto`, `always`, or `never`\n+- `auto_flush_vram_threshold_gb`\n+- `port`\n+- `extra`: raw HiddenSwitch configuration keys not represented by typed fields\n+\n+`EmbeddedSession` holds one `Comfy()` context across multiple `run()` calls. `ServerSession` holds one `comfyui serve` subprocess and uses HTTP for readiness, prompt queueing, and explicit flush.\n*** End Patch\n"
}
