        Idea:
vibecomfy: warm-runtime sessions (keep models resident across generations by reusing Comfy's existing model_management, not reinventing it).

Source repo: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/vibecomfy
Embedded ComfyUI fork in use: hiddenswitch (path: /Users/user_c042661f/.cache/uv/git-v0/checkouts/b790d0c34622492a/c5ed9402/)

# Problem
Today both vibecomfy runtime paths are cold-per-call:
- Embedded: vibecomfy/runtime/run.py:125-138 enters/exits a fresh Comfy() context per run. The exit calls embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup -> model_management.unload_all_models() + gc + soft_empty_cache. Models are dumped after every generation.
- Managed server: vibecomfy/runtime/server.py:16 starts comfyui as a subprocess, queues once, terminates. Same outcome at the process level.

ComfyUI itself already keeps models warm by default — current_loaded_models persists in process, load_models_gpu reuses resident copies, eviction is pressure-driven, OOM is auto-handled (execution.py:735-737), and DISABLE_SMART_MEMORY is the only thing that forces unload after each prompt. So the work is structural: stop tearing the runtime down.

# Goal
Add session-aware warm runtime to vibecomfy with two backends sharing one abstraction:
1. Long-lived managed Comfy server (HTTP control plane) — default for CLI users.
2. Long-lived embedded Comfy() context — for in-process callers (runpod worker, tests, tight matrix loops).

Default policy: keep warm. Explicit flush available. Auto-flush on detected model-set change under VRAM pressure.

# Verified facts (don't re-research)
- POST /free and POST /api/free both exist in the embedded hiddenswitch fork at comfy/cmd/server.py:1041 with /api mirror auto-added at :1357. Payload: {"unload_models": bool, "free_memory": bool}. They set queue flags via prompt_queue.set_flag(...) — async, take effect at next prompt boundary, not synchronously.
- CLI flags exist in comfy/cli_args.py:115-160: --highvram, --lowvram, --normalvram, --reserve-vram <GB>, --disable-smart-memory, --cache-classic, --cache-lru N, --cache-none.
- Comfy.clear_cache() (embedded_comfy_client.py:385) and Comfy.reconfigure() (:398) already exposed; reconfigure uses MODEL_MANAGEMENT_ARGS fingerprint to avoid unnecessary subprocess cycling.
- The cleanup that nukes warm state lives in embedded_comfy_client.py:_cleanup (lines ~149-169) and is called from Comfy.__aexit__ (line ~481).

# Design constraints / refinements over the user's notes
- Do NOT default to --highvram (breaks multi-model workflows that need offload). Default to Comfy's smart memory.
- /free is asynchronous — flush API must either drain via a no-op prompt or document the queue-flag semantics. Tests must account for this.
- Auto-flush should only trigger on model-set fingerprint change AND VRAM-headroom-below-threshold. Input/seed/prompt changes must NOT trigger flush.
- VIBECOMFY_WARM=auto|always|never controls policy (auto default).
- Session lifecycle: daemon-style for CLI (pidfile + URL file under out/sessions/<id>/) so multiple `vibecomfy run` invocations share one warm pool. Embedded session is just a Python context manager.
- Browser-only tasks: there are none in this plan (no UI work).
- No hard-halt prereq clauses: each milestone is independently shippable.

# Out of scope for this megaplan
- Multi-GPU session pooling.
- Distributed/remote session sharing across machines.
- Workflow-graph-level caching (Comfy already has --cache-lru / --cache-classic).
- Replacing the existing one-shot run/run_embedded APIs — they should remain as thin wrappers around the new session.
- Cloud/RunPod orchestration changes (the runpod worker can adopt EmbeddedSession in a separate plan).

# Suggested milestone shape (megaplan can refine during prep/plan)
M1: Session abstraction + embedded backend
  - vibecomfy/runtime/session.py: VibeSession protocol + EmbeddedSession holding a long-lived Comfy() across multiple .run(workflow) calls.
  - .flush() -> Comfy.clear_cache(); .reconfigure(config) -> Comfy.reconfigure().
  - Refactor run_embedded() to be a one-shot wrapper around EmbeddedSession.
  - Tests: two sequential runs in one session reuse models (assert via inspecting comfy.model_management.current_loaded_models after each run, or via a stub model manager); flush() drops them; reconfigure with same fingerprint is a no-op.

M2: Managed server backend
  - ServerSession: keeps comfy_server subprocess alive across .run() calls. Start = spawn + wait-ready. .flush() = POST /api/free with appropriate flags. .stop() = SIGTERM with timeout fallback to SIGKILL.
  - Daemon mode: `vibecomfy session start [--id NAME] [--vram-policy ...]` writes pidfile + URL to out/sessions/<id>/; `vibecomfy session stop <id>`; `vibecomfy session list`; `vibecomfy session flush <id>`.
  - `vibecomfy run` auto-discovers active session if one exists, else falls back to one-shot managed mode.
  - Tests: session start/stop, flush via HTTP, two runs share warmth, daemon survives across CLI invocations.

M3: Auto-flush policy + fingerprinting
  - vibecomfy/runtime/model_fingerprint.py: extract loader-node identity tuples from a workflow (CheckpointLoaderSimple.ckpt_name, LoraLoader.lora_name, UNETLoader.unet_name, VAELoader.vae_name, CLIPLoader.clip_name, DualCLIPLoader, etc. — discover the canonical set from the embedded fork's nodes).
  - Session.run() compares current workflow fingerprint to last-run fingerprint. If different AND free VRAM < threshold (configurable, default e.g. 2GB), call flush() before queueing.
  - VIBECOMFY_WARM env var: auto (default) | always | never.
  - Tests: same model + different seed -> no flush; different ckpt -> flush; auto-flush gated by VRAM headroom (mock get_free_memory).

M4: VRAM/cache flag plumbing + docs
  - Session config accepts: vram_policy ("auto"|"high"|"low"|"normal"), reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy ("smart"|"classic"|"lru:N"|"none"), disable_smart_memory.
  - Threaded through to embedded Configuration AND server CLI args.
  - docs/runtime_lifecycle.md: add "Warm sessions" section. docs/runtime_surface.md: document VibeSession API.
  - Smoke test: `vibecomfy session start && vibecomfy run x.json && vibecomfy run y.json && vibecomfy session stop` — second run is materially faster than first (timing-based, with tolerance).

# Open questions for the planning step
- Should `vibecomfy run` lazily start a session on first call and reuse it (implicit warm) vs always require `session start`? Lean toward explicit start for predictability.
- Does the runpod worker (reigh-workspace/reigh-worker) want to consume EmbeddedSession directly or talk to a local managed session? Probably embedded — it owns the process lifecycle already. Out of scope for this plan, but the API should not preclude it.
- Do we need partial flush (just CLIP, just VAE, just UNet)? Probably no — Comfy's pressure-driven eviction handles per-model decisions; only expose all-or-nothing flush at the session boundary.

# Success criteria (suggested — planning phase should refine)
- A `VibeSession` abstraction exists with both Embedded and Server backends.
- Two sequential `.run()` calls in one session share warm models (verified by test, not just timing).
- `flush()` is explicit and works for both backends. For the server backend, the test accounts for /free's queue-flag semantics.
- `vibecomfy session start/stop/list/flush` CLI commands exist and survive across separate `vibecomfy` invocations.
- Auto-flush policy fires on model-set change under VRAM pressure, never on seed/input changes.
- Existing `run` and `run_embedded` one-shot APIs continue to work unchanged (backwards compatibility).
- Documentation updated in docs/runtime_lifecycle.md and docs/runtime_surface.md.

User notes and answers:
- FLAG-012 fix: _partition_comfy_config must accept both typed SessionConfig field names and raw HiddenSwitch keys. Treat typed names (port, vram_policy, cache_policy, warm_policy, reserve_vram_gb) as direct routes to typed dataclass fields. Treat raw HS keys (reserve_vram, cache_none, --highvram, etc.) via the existing HiddenSwitch translation path. Unknown keys go to extra. Tests in Step 7.3 expect typed-name daemon config carry-through; tests for the embedded path may also feed raw HS keys.

Daemon body: `config_dict = json.loads(args.config); session = ServerSession(SessionConfig.from_dict(config_dict)); await session.start(); write out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}; install SIGTERM handler that calls await session.stop() and removes pidfile; sleep on asyncio.Event`. Daemonize from `session start` via `subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-m', 'vibecomfy.commands.session', '--daemon', '--id', id_, '--config', json.dumps(config)], stdout=DEVNULL, stderr=open(log_path,'ab'), start_new_session=True)`. Parent assembles config dict from CLI flags as TYPED-NAME dict matching SessionConfig field names (omit None values): `{port, vram_policy, reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy, warm_policy, disable_smart_memory}`. Parent waits up to 120 s for out/sessions/<id>/url. Subcommands: `start [--id NAME=default] [--vram-policy auto|high|low|normal] [--reserve-vram-gb F] [--cache-policy smart|classic|lru:N|none] [--disable-smart-memory] [--warm-policy auto|always|never] [--port P=8188]`, `stop <id>`, `list`, `flush <id> [--unload-models/--no-unload-models] [--free-memory/--no-free-memory]`, `status <id>`.       "id": "T2",
      "description": "Add the auto-flush gate inside EmbeddedSession.run in session.py. Before queueing: compute fp = model_fingerprint(api_dict). Resolve effective warm policy: os.environ.get('VIBECOMFY_WARM') overrides config.warm_policy. Behavior: 'never' \u2192 flush() then queue; 'always' \u2192 never auto-flush; 'auto' \u2192 flush iff `last_fingerprint is not None and fp != last_fingerprint and _free_vram_gb() < config.auto_flush_vram_threshold_gb`. After successful queue: self.last_fingerprint = fp. Add `_free_vram_gb()` helper that lazily imports `comfy.model_management.get_free_memory()` and returns float('inf') on any ImportError/AttributeError so missing GPU/torch doesn't break the gate.",
      "depends_on": [

      "status": "done",
      "executor_notes": "Added the EmbeddedSession.run auto-flush gate. Added _free_vram_gb() with lazy comfy.model_management import and float('inf') fallback on ImportError/AttributeError. Verified with a FakeComfy trace for same fingerprint + low VRAM, changed fingerprint + low VRAM, changed fingerprint + high VRAM, same loader + different seed, VIBECOMFY_WARM=never, and VIBECOMFY_WARM=always.       "description": "Create vibecomfy/commands/session.py with argparse for start/stop/list/flush/status AND in-process daemon entrypoint (single file). Daemon invocation: `python -m vibecomfy.commands.session --daemon --id NAME --config <json>`. Daemon body: `config_dict = json.loads(args.config); session = ServerSession(SessionConfig.from_dict(config_dict)); await session.start(); write out/sessions/<id>/{pid,url,config.json}; install SIGTERM handler that calls await session.stop() and removes pidfile; sleep on asyncio.Event`. Daemonize from `session start` via `subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, '-m', 'vibecomfy.commands.session', '--daemon', '--id', id_, '--config', json.dumps(config)], stdout=DEVNULL, stderr=open(log_path,'ab'), start_new_session=True)`. Parent assembles config dict from CLI flags as TYPED-NAME dict matching SessionConfig field names (omit None values): `{port, vram_policy, reserve_vram_gb, cache_policy, warm_policy, disable_smart_memory}`. Parent waits up to 120 s for out/sessions/<id>/url. Subcommands: `start [--id NAME=default] [--vram-policy auto|high|low|normal] [--reserve-vram-gb F] [--cache-policy smart|classic|lru:N|none] [--disable-smart-memory] [--warm-policy auto|always|never] [--port P=8188]`, `stop <id>`, `list`, `flush <id> [--unload-models/--no-unload-models] [--free-memory/--no-free-memory]`, `status <id>`. Add `find_active_session(id='default') -> str | None` in runtime/session.py: read out/sessions/<id>/url; if pidfile present and os.kill(pid, 0) succeeds return URL else clean up and return None. Register CommandSpec('session', 'vibecomfy.commands.session') in vibecomfy/commands/__init__.py:14-28 BETWEEN 'runtime' and 'logs'. Update tests/test_cli.py:25-40 to insert 'session' between 'runtime' and 'logs' in the asserted ordered list. Make warm-managed default for `vibecomfy run` (vibecomfy/commands/run.py): change --runtime default from 'embedded' to 'auto'; at top of _cmd_run BEFORE get_schema_provider, when args.runtime in ('auto','server') and --server-url not set, call find_active_session('default'); resolve effective runtime (auto + warm \u2192 server@URL, auto + no warm \u2192 embedded one-shot, server + warm \u2192 reuse, server + no warm \u2192 existing one-shot managed).   "meta_commentary": "Plan size is large (~10 tasks across three phases). Recommended execution order matches plan validation order: Phase 1 first (T1\u2192T2\u2192T3\u2192T4), then Phase 2 (T5\u2192T6\u2192T7), then docs (T8) and smoke (T9), then T10 to validate. Critical correctness traps to remember while executing: (1) The _partition_comfy_config dual-vocabulary contract is the single most-flagged correctness item across five gate iterations \u2014 both typed SessionConfig field names AND raw HiddenSwitch keys must be accepted in one dict; the typed-vs-HS conflict test (T4 case 8) and the daemon carry-through test (T7) are the canonical assertions. Get this wrong and the FLAG-012 work was wasted. (2) _embedded_configuration MUST keep its `(workflow: VibeWorkflow) -> Configuration` signature unchanged. The new logic lives in _embedded_configuration_for_session(SessionConfig); the workflow-taking helper is a one-line wrapper that routes through SessionConfig.from_workflow_metadata. Don't change the public signature. (3) Helper relocation is one-way (run.py imports session.py, never the reverse). The circular import was specifically called out and resolved \u2014 don't reintroduce it. (4) Pattern-based fingerprint, NOT a hard-coded class list. Anything containing 'Loader' (and not in OVERRIDES_EXCLUDE) qualifies. Edge references (list values) are excluded \u2014 string values only. (5) Warm-default routing: --runtime default flips from 'embedded' to 'auto'. The schema-provider URL must be threaded BEFORE get_schema_provider construction so a second cold runtime doesn't spawn for /object_info. Both run_sync AND get_schema_provider receive the same URL. (6) test_cli.py:25-40 has an exact-ordered COMMANDS assertion \u2014 it WILL break when 'session' is registered unless updated in the same change. (7) /api/free is queue-async; tests assert payload, not synchronous unload completion. (8) Daemon spawn uses start_new_session=True; pidfile + URL files under out/sessions/<id>/. find_active_session cleans up stale pidfiles when os.kill(pid, 0) raises. (9) GPU smoke (T9) is info-only; the dev mac can't run it. The test layer (spawn count == 1 + /api/free payload) is the real warmth-verification gate. Read tests/test_runtime_run.py:140-149 first to copy the sys.modules injection pattern \u2014 all new tests use it. The plan went through 5 critique iterations; trust the settled decisions in the plan body and don't second-guess them.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution.
